`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/FaceRegionTests.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// The outline Face Pattern clips to (patterns spec §3): even-odd containment, the distance to every edge, the inset guide.
struct FaceRegionTests {
    func square(_ low: Double, _ high: Double) -> [Vector2] {
        [Vector2(low, low), Vector2(high, low), Vector2(high, high), Vector2(low, high)]
    }

    var plate: FaceRegion { FaceRegion(loops: [square(0, 40)]) }
    var drilled: FaceRegion { FaceRegion(loops: [square(0, 40), square(15, 25)]) }

    @Test func aPointIsOnTheFaceInsideTheOutlineAndOutsideItsHoles() {
        #expect(plate.contains(Vector2(20, 20)) && !plate.contains(Vector2(41, 20)) && !plate.contains(Vector2(-1, 20)))
        #expect(drilled.contains(Vector2(5, 5)) && !drilled.contains(Vector2(20, 20)))
    }

    @Test func theDistanceToTheBoundaryCountsHolesToo() {
        #expect(isClose(plate.distanceToBoundary(Vector2(10, 20)), 10))
        #expect(isClose(drilled.distanceToBoundary(Vector2(10, 20)), 5), "the hole is nearer than the outline")
        #expect(isClose(plate.distanceToBoundary(Vector2(-3, 20)), 3), "measured from outside, too")
    }

    @Test func aMarginKeepsPlacementsClearOfEveryEdge() {
        #expect(plate.allows(Vector2(3, 20), margin: 3) && !plate.allows(Vector2(2.9, 20), margin: 3))
        #expect(drilled.allows(Vector2(12, 20), margin: 3) && !drilled.allows(Vector2(13, 20), margin: 3))
        #expect(plate.allows(Vector2(0.5, 20), margin: 0), "no margin only needs the face")
        #expect(!plate.allows(Vector2(-0.5, 20), margin: 0))
    }

    @Test func theBoundsEnclosEveryLoop() throws {
        let box = try #require(drilled.bounds)
        #expect(box.min == Vector2(0, 0) && box.max == Vector2(40, 40))
        #expect(FaceRegion(loops: []).bounds == nil)
    }

    @Test func aLoopOfFewerThanThreePointsIsNotAnOutline() {
        #expect(FaceRegion(loops: [[Vector2(0, 0), Vector2(1, 1)]]).loops.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func theInsetGuideMovesTheOutlineInAndHolesOut() {
        let guide = drilled.insetOutline(margin: 3)
        func sorted(_ loop: [Vector2]) -> [Vector2] { loop.sorted { ($0.x, $0.y) < ($1.x, $1.y) } }
        #expect(sorted(guide[0]) == sorted(square(3, 37)))
        #expect(sorted(guide[1]) == sorted(square(12, 28)))
    }

    @Test func theInsetGuideDoesNotDependOnWhichWayALoopRuns() {
        let reversed = FaceRegion(loops: [square(0, 40).reversed(), square(15, 25).reversed()])
        let guide = reversed.insetOutline(margin: 3)
        #expect(Set(guide[0].map(\.x)) == [3, 37] && Set(guide[1].map(\.x)) == [12, 28])
    }

    @Test func noMarginGivesTheOutlineItself() {
        #expect(drilled.insetOutline(margin: 0) == drilled.loops)
    }
}
```

`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/FacePatternNodeTests.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Foundation
import Testing

/// Face Pattern (patterns spec §3): a grid on a picked flat face, clipped to the face, its holes and an edge margin.
struct FacePatternNodeTests {
    /// A Face Pattern on the top of the 60 × 40 × 6 plate (z = 0), with a Ø20 hole through its middle when `drilled`.
    struct Fixture {
        var plate: PatternPlate
        let pattern: Node
        let part: Node

        init(drilled: Bool = false, hole diameter: Double = 20, _ settings: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) {
            plate = PatternPlate()
            pattern = plate.h.add(FacePatternNode.self, settings)
            if drilled {
                let hole = plate.cylinder(diameter: diameter, from: -7, to: 1)
                let cut = plate.h.add(BooleanNode.self, ["operation": .integer(1)])
                plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: cut, "target")
                plate.h.wire(hole, "solid", to: cut, "tools")
                part = cut
            } else {
                part = plate.plate
            }
            plate.h.wire(part, "solid", to: pattern, "solid")
        }

        /// Picks the face of the part with the given outward normal (the top is +Z) and returns the report of the pattern.
        mutating func pick(normal: Vector3 = .unitZ, kernel: any Kernel = OCCTKernel()) async throws -> EvaluationReport {
            let solid = try onlySolid(try await plate.h.run([part], kernel: kernel), part)
            let face = try #require(solid.topology.faces.filter { $0.kind == .plane && $0.normal.map { isClose($0, normal) } == true }
                .max { $0.centroid.dot(normal) < $1.centroid.dot(normal) })
            plate.h.set(pattern, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(try #require(solid.topology.facePick(for: face.id))))
            return try await plate.h.run([pattern], kernel: kernel)
        }

        /// `value` to a micrometre: OCCT's centroids carry noise of 1e-16.
        static func snapped(_ value: Double) -> Double { (value * 1e6).rounded() / 1e6 + 0 }

        func tree(_ report: EvaluationReport) throws -> DataTree {
            try #require(report.value(pattern, "placements")?.asTree, "\(String(describing: report.state(pattern)))")
        }

        /// The placements of each row, empty cells left out: row `j` is the `j`th row the face's box reaches.
        func rows(_ report: EvaluationReport) throws -> [[Plane]] {
            let leaves = try tree(report).leaves
            return Dictionary(grouping: leaves, by: { $0.path.indices[0] }).sorted { $0.key < $1.key }.map { _, cells in
                cells.flatMap { cell in cell.items.compactMap { if case .plane(let plane) = $0 { plane } else { nil } } }
            }
        }

        /// Where each placed vent is, by its path `{row;column;0}`.
        func positions(_ report: EvaluationReport) throws -> [[Int]: Vector2] {
            var found: [[Int]: Vector2] = [:]
            for leaf in try tree(report).leaves {
                for (index, item) in leaf.items.enumerated() {
                    if case .plane(let plane) = item {
                        found[leaf.path.indices + [index]] = Vector2(Fixture.snapped(plane.origin.x), Fixture.snapped(plane.origin.y))
                    }
                }
            }
            return found
        }
    }

    @Test func aGridFillsTheFaceKeepingTheMarginFromItsEdges() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        let report = try await f.pick()
        let rows = try f.rows(report)
        // The face is 60 × 40 about its centroid (0, 0, 0). With a margin of 3 only |x| <= 27 and |y| <= 17 qualify: columns
        // −20…20 in rows −10…10, and the rows at y = ±20 (reached by the face's box, kept as branches of empty cells) are empty.
        #expect(rows.map(\.count) == [0, 5, 5, 5, 0])
        let positions = try f.positions(report)
        // Rows are counted from the lowest row the box reaches (y = −20) and columns from the lowest column (x = −30), so
        // the vent at (−20, −10) is {1;1;0} and the one at (20, 10) is {3;5;0}.
        #expect(positions[[1, 1, 0]] == Vector2(-20, -10) && positions[[3, 5, 0]] == Vector2(20, 10) && positions.count == 15)
        #expect(rows[1].map { Fixture.snapped($0.origin.x) } == [-20, -10, 0, 10, 20])
        #expect(rows.map { $0.first.map { Fixture.snapped($0.origin.y) } } == [nil, -10, 0, 10, nil])
        #expect(rows.flatMap { $0 }.allSatisfy { $0.normal == .unitZ && isClose($0.origin.z, 0) && $0.xAxis == .unitX })
    }

    @Test func staggerMovesEverySecondRowHalfAPitch() async throws {
        var f = Fixture(["stagger": .bool(true)])
        let rows = try f.rows(try await f.pick())
        #expect(rows.map(\.count) == [0, 6, 5, 6, 0])
        #expect(rows[1].map { Fixture.snapped($0.origin.x) } == [-25, -15, -5, 5, 15, 25])
        #expect(rows[2].map { Fixture.snapped($0.origin.x) } == [-20, -10, 0, 10, 20])
    }

    @Test func aHoleInTheFaceAddsItsOwnMargin() async throws {
        var f = Fixture(drilled: true)
        let rows = try f.rows(try await f.pick())
        let kept = rows.flatMap { $0 }.map { Vector2(Fixture.snapped($0.origin.x), Fixture.snapped($0.origin.y)) }
        // Every grid position on the face that is 3 mm clear of the outline and of the Ø20 hole (13 mm from the centre).
        var expected: [Vector2] = []
        for y in stride(from: -10.0, through: 10, by: 10) {
            for x in stride(from: -20.0, through: 20, by: 10) where Vector2(x, y).length >= 13 { expected.append(Vector2(x, y)) }
        }
        #expect(Set(kept) == Set(expected) && kept.count == 10, "\(kept)")
        #expect(!kept.contains(Vector2(0, 0)) && !kept.contains(Vector2(10, 0)))
    }

    @Test func aBiggerMarginDropsTheOuterColumnsAndRows() async throws {
        var f = Fixture(["margin": .number(12)])
        let rows = try f.rows(try await f.pick())
        #expect(rows.map(\.count) == [0, 0, 3, 0, 0], "|x| <= 18 and |y| <= 8")
    }

    /// Patterns spec §6: a pick on a vent follows the vent. A margin, a hole or a spacing of a hole changes which cells are
    /// clipped, never the path of a cell that stays (a path counted among the placements kept would shift every one after a gap).
    @Test func aVentKeepsItsPathWhenTheMarginGrowsByAPitch() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        let before = try f.positions(try await f.pick())
        f.plate.h.set(f.pattern, "margin", .number(13))
        let after = try f.positions(try await f.plate.h.run([f.pattern], kernel: OCCTKernel()))
        #expect(before.count == 15 && after.count == 3, "|x| <= 17 and |y| <= 7: three vents on the middle row")
        #expect(after.allSatisfy { before[$0.key] == $0.value }, "\(after) against \(before)")
        #expect(after[[2, 2, 0]] == Vector2(-10, 0) && after[[2, 4, 0]] == Vector2(10, 0))
    }

    @Test func aVentKeepsItsPathWhenAHoleGrows() async throws {
        var small = Fixture(drilled: true)
        var large = Fixture(drilled: true, hole: 30)
        let before = try small.positions(try await small.pick())
        let after = try large.positions(try await large.pick())
        #expect(before.count == 10 && after.count == 6, "a Ø30 hole takes the vents within 18 mm of the centre")
        #expect(after.allSatisfy { before[$0.key] == $0.value }, "\(after) against \(before)")
    }

    @Test func aFaceNarrowerThanHalfAPitchStaggersWithoutImpossibleColumns() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let slab = h.box(4, 40, 6, at: Vector3(0, 0, -6))
        let pattern = h.add(FacePatternNode.self, ["stagger": .bool(true), "margin": .number(0)])
        h.wire(slab, "solid", to: pattern, "solid")
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let solid = try onlySolid(try await h.run([slab], kernel: kernel), slab)
        let top = try #require(solid.topology.faces.first { $0.kind == .plane && $0.normal == .unitZ })
        h.set(pattern, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(try #require(solid.topology.facePick(for: top.id))))
        let report = try await h.run([pattern], kernel: kernel)
        let tree = try #require(report.value(pattern, "placements")?.asTree, "\(String(describing: report.state(pattern)))")
        let origins = tree.items.compactMap { item -> Vector3? in if case .plane(let plane) = item { plane.origin } else { nil } }
        #expect(!origins.isEmpty && origins.allSatisfy { abs($0.x) < 1e-6 }, "only the unstaggered rows reach a 4 mm face")
    }

    @Test func aSideFaceFacesOutAlongItsNormal() async throws {
        var f = Fixture(["margin": .number(1)])
        let rows = try f.rows(try await f.pick(normal: .unitX))
        let placements = rows.flatMap { $0 }
        #expect(!placements.isEmpty)
        #expect(placements.allSatisfy { $0.normal == .unitX && isClose($0.origin.x, 30) })
    }

    @Test func aCurvedFaceIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let circle = h.add(CircleNode.self, ["diameter": .number(20)])
        let extrude = h.add(ExtrudeNode.self, ["distance": .number(10)])
        let pattern = h.add(FacePatternNode.self)
        h.wire(circle, "profile", to: extrude, "profile")
        h.wire(extrude, "solid", to: pattern, "solid")
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let solid = try onlySolid(try await h.run([extrude], kernel: kernel), extrude)
        let side = try #require(solid.topology.faces.first { $0.kind == .cylinder })
        h.set(pattern, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(try #require(solid.topology.facePick(for: side.id))))
        #expect(try await h.run([pattern], kernel: kernel).error(pattern) == "Face Pattern works on flat faces for now.")
    }

    @Test func noPickOrAPickThatIsGoneSaysSo() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        let none = try await f.plate.h.run([f.pattern], kernel: OCCTKernel())
        #expect(none.error(f.pattern) == "Pick a face for this pattern to fill.")
        let stranger = FacePick(tags: [TopoTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0, role: .endCap)])
        f.plate.h.set(f.pattern, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(stranger))
        let gone = try await f.plate.h.run([f.pattern], kernel: OCCTKernel())
        #expect(gone.error(f.pattern) == "The picked face isn't on this solid any more. Pick it again.")
        f.plate.h.set(f.pattern, NodeSetting.face, .bool(true))
        #expect(try await f.plate.h.run([f.pattern], kernel: OCCTKernel()).error(f.pattern)
            == "This pattern's picked face can't be read. Pick the face again.")
    }

    @Test(arguments: [
        (["spacingX": .number(0)], "“spacingX” must be greater than 0 mm."),
        (["spacingY": .number(-1)], "“spacingY” must be greater than 0 mm."),
        (["margin": .number(-1)], "“margin” must be 0 or more."),
        (["spacingX": .number(0.5), "spacingY": .number(0.5)], "Patterns are limited to 2,000 instances."),
        (["spacingX": .number(0.01), "spacingY": .number(0.01)],
         "Face Pattern would have to try more than 200,000 positions: raise the spacing."),
    ] as [([SocketName: ConstantValue], String)])
    func aBadInputIsRefusedInPlainWords(settings: [SocketName: ConstantValue], message: String) async throws {
        var f = Fixture(settings)
        #expect(try await f.pick().error(f.pattern) == message)
    }

    @Test func theClippedAreaForThePreviewIsTheFaceMovedInByTheMargin() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        _ = try await f.pick()
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let solid = try onlySolid(try await f.plate.h.run([f.part], kernel: kernel), f.part)
        guard case .facePick(let pick)? = f.plate.h.nodes[f.pattern.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.face] else {
            Issue.record("no pick stored")
            return
        }
        let loops = FacePatternNode.clippedArea(of: solid, pick: pick, margin: 3)
        #expect(loops.count == 1)
        let loop = try #require(loops.first)
        #expect(Set(loop.map { Vector2($0.x, $0.y) }) == [Vector2(-27, -17), Vector2(27, -17), Vector2(27, 17), Vector2(-27, 17)])
        #expect(loop.allSatisfy { isClose($0.z, 0) })
        #expect(FacePatternNode.clippedArea(of: solid, pick: FacePick(tags: []), margin: 3).isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aPlaceOverTheFacePatternCopiesToolsInTheTreeOfCells() async throws {
        var f = Fixture()
        _ = try await f.pick()
        let tool = f.plate.cylinder(diameter: 4, from: 0, to: 3)
        let place = f.plate.h.add(PlaceNode.self)
        f.plate.h.wire(f.pattern, "placements", to: place, "placements")
        f.plate.h.wire(tool, "solid", to: place, "tool")
        let report = try await f.plate.h.run([place], kernel: FakeKernel())
        let tree = try #require(report.value(place, "solids")?.asTree, "\(String(describing: report.state(place)))")
        #expect(tree.depth == 3 && tree.itemCount == 15, "a cell is a branch of one copy, an empty cell a branch of none")
        let perRow = Dictionary(grouping: tree.leaves, by: { $0.path.indices[0] }).sorted { $0.key < $1.key }
        #expect(perRow.map { $0.value.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.items.count } } == [0, 5, 5, 5, 0])
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Layouts/FaceRegion.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import Foundation

/// The outline of a flat face in the face's own 2D coordinates (Face Pattern, patterns spec §3): its outer loop and each hole as
/// closed polylines (arcs cut into chords no more than `chordTolerance` from the arc). A point is in the face by the even-odd
/// rule over all the loops, so a hole needs no special case.
struct FaceRegion {
    /// How far a chord may lie from the arc it stands for, in millimetres.
    static let chordTolerance = 0.01

    /// Closed loops, the last point not repeating the first.
    let loops: [[Vector2]]

    init(loops: [[Vector2]]) {
        self.loops = loops.filter { $0.count >= 3 }
    }

    /// The corners of the box round every loop; `nil` for a face with no outline.
    var bounds: (min: Vector2, max: Vector2)? {
        let points = loops.joined()
        guard let first = points.first else { return nil }
        return points.reduce((first, first)) { box, p in
            (Vector2(Swift.min(box.0.x, p.x), Swift.min(box.0.y, p.y)), Vector2(Swift.max(box.1.x, p.x), Swift.max(box.1.y, p.y)))
        }
    }

    /// Whether `point` is on the face: inside an odd number of loops.
    func contains(_ point: Vector2) -> Bool {
        var inside = false
        for loop in loops {
            var previous = loop[loop.count - 1]
            for current in loop {
                if (current.y > point.y) != (previous.y > point.y),
                   point.x < (previous.x - current.x) * (point.y - current.y) / (previous.y - current.y) + current.x {
                    inside.toggle()
                }
                previous = current
            }
        }
        return inside
    }

    /// The distance from `point` to the nearest edge of any loop.
    func distanceToBoundary(_ point: Vector2) -> Double {
        var nearest = Double.infinity
        for loop in loops {
            var previous = loop[loop.count - 1]
            for current in loop {
                nearest = Swift.min(nearest, Self.distance(from: point, toSegment: previous, current))
                previous = current
            }
        }
        return nearest
    }

    /// Whether a placement may stand at `point`: on the face and at least `margin` from every edge, holes' included.
    func allows(_ point: Vector2, margin: Double) -> Bool {
        contains(point) && (margin <= 0 || distanceToBoundary(point) >= margin - 1e-9)
    }

    private static func distance(from p: Vector2, toSegment a: Vector2, _ b: Vector2) -> Double {
        let along = b - a
        let lengthSquared = along.x * along.x + along.y * along.y
        guard lengthSquared > 0 else { return (p - a).length }
        let t = Swift.min(Swift.max(((p.x - a.x) * along.x + (p.y - a.y) * along.y) / lengthSquared, 0), 1)
        return (p - (a + along * t)).length
    }

    /// The edge of the area placements may stand in, for the viewport: each loop moved `margin` into the face, corners
    /// mitred (and the mitre held to four margins, so a sharp corner doesn't fly off). Exact on straight edges; at an arc or a
    /// sharp corner only a guide, since placements are kept clear of the edge by distance, not by this outline.
    func insetOutline(margin: Double) -> [[Vector2]] {
        guard margin > 0 else { return loops }
        return loops.enumerated().map { index, loop in
            let area = Self.signedArea(loop)
            // A loop inside an odd number of others is a hole: the face lies outside it. Counter-clockwise puts the inside on the left.
            let enclosing = loops.indices.filter { $0 != index && Self.contains(loops[$0], loop[0]) }.count
            let faceSide = (enclosing.isMultiple(of: 2) ? 1.0 : -1.0) * (area >= 0 ? 1.0 : -1.0)
            return Self.mitred(loop, by: margin * faceSide)
        }
    }

    private static func signedArea(_ loop: [Vector2]) -> Double {
        var sum = 0.0
        var previous = loop[loop.count - 1]
        for current in loop {
            sum += previous.x * current.y - current.x * previous.y
            previous = current
        }
        return sum / 2
    }

    private static func contains(_ loop: [Vector2], _ point: Vector2) -> Bool {
        FaceRegion(loops: [loop]).contains(point)
    }

    /// `loop` moved by `offset` along the left normal of each edge (negative: the right), corners where the moved edges meet.
    private static func mitred(_ loop: [Vector2], by offset: Double) -> [Vector2] {
        let count = loop.count
        let edges = (0..<count).map { index -> (point: Vector2, direction: Vector2)? in
            let delta = loop[(index + 1) % count] - loop[index]
            let length = delta.length
            guard length > 1e-12 else { return nil }
            let direction = delta * (1 / length)
            return (loop[index] + Vector2(-direction.y, direction.x) * offset, direction)
        }
        return (0..<count).map { index in
            let corner = loop[index]
            guard let before = edges[(index + count - 1) % count], let after = edges[index] else { return corner }
            let cross = before.direction.x * after.direction.y - before.direction.y * after.direction.x
            guard abs(cross) > 1e-9 else { return corner + (after.point - loop[index]) }
            // Where the line through `before` meets the line through `after`.
            let gap = after.point - before.point
            let t = (gap.x * after.direction.y - gap.y * after.direction.x) / cross
            let meeting = before.point + before.direction * t
            let shift = meeting - corner
            return shift.length > 4 * abs(offset) ? corner + shift * (4 * abs(offset) / shift.length) : meeting
        }
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Layouts/FaceRegion+Face.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Foundation

extension FaceRegion {
    static let unreadable = "Face Pattern can't read this face's outline: it has an edge that is neither straight nor circular."

    /// The outline of the flat face `face` of `topology`, in 2D coordinates on the plane through `origin` spanned by the unit
    /// vectors `xAxis` and `yAxis`. Its boundary edges (seams aside) are joined into loops (`EdgeChains`); a face whose edges are
    /// not all straight or circular, or do not close up, can't be read and refuses.
    static func outline(of face: FaceInfo, in topology: Topology, origin: Vector3, xAxis: Vector3, yAxis: Vector3) throws -> FaceRegion {
        let edges = topology.edges.filter { !$0.isSeam && $0.faces.contains(face.id) }
        let curves = edges.compactMap(\.curve)
        guard !edges.isEmpty, curves.count == edges.count else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(unreadable) }
        let chains = EdgeChains.chains(of: curves)
        guard chains.allSatisfy(\.isClosed) else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(unreadable) }
        let loops = chains.map { chain in
            chain.pieces.flatMap { polyline(of: $0) }.map { point in
                Vector2((point - origin).dot(xAxis), (point - origin).dot(yAxis))
            }
        }
        return FaceRegion(loops: loops)
    }

    /// The points that stand for `piece`, from its start up to but not including its end: just the start for a line, chords for an arc.
    private static func polyline(of piece: EdgeCurve) -> [Vector3] {
        guard case .circle(_, _, let radius, _, let sweep) = piece, radius > 0 else { return [piece.startPoint] }
        let step = chordTolerance < radius ? 2 * acos(1 - chordTolerance / radius) : .pi
        let chords = min(max(Int((abs(sweep) / step).rounded(.up)), 1), 720)
        return (0..<chords).compactMap { piece.sample(at: piece.pathLength * Double($0) / Double(chords))?.point }
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorNodes/Patterns/Layouts/FacePatternNode.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Placements filling a flat face (patterns spec §3): a grid of `spacingX` by `spacingY` laid out on the face (the plane
/// Plane from Face would give: through the face's centroid, x axis from `FacePlane`), every second row moved half a pitch
/// with `stagger` (hex-style), each placement facing out along the face's normal. Only positions on the face are kept, and at
/// least `margin` from every edge of it, the edges of its holes included. The face is a remembered `FacePick`, the same as
/// Plane from Face's, so it follows the model.
///
/// One branch per grid cell, in a branch per row: a placement is `{row;column;0}`, the row counted from the lowest row the face's
/// box reaches and the column from the lowest column it reaches (a stagger's half pitch included), and a cell that is clipped
/// (off the face, in a hole or inside the margin) is an empty branch. So a vent keeps its path while the margin, a hole or the
/// spacing of a hole changes, and the cells that stay keep their names; only a face that grows past its box renumbers.
/// A curved face refuses ("Face Pattern works on flat faces for now."). More than 2,000 placements refuses.
public enum FacePatternNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.facePattern"
    public static let displayName = "Face Pattern"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.patterns
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("solid", .solid),
        SocketSpec("spacingX", .number, defaultValue: .number(10), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.5...200),
        SocketSpec("spacingY", .number, defaultValue: .number(10), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.5...200),
        SocketSpec("stagger", .bool, defaultValue: .bool(false)),
        SocketSpec("margin", .number, defaultValue: .number(3), unit: .millimetres, range: 0...50),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("placements", .plane)]
    public static var singleValuesRefusal: String? { LayoutChecks.singleValues(displayName) }
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Grid", controls: [.slider("spacingX"), .slider("spacingY"), .toggle("stagger", label: "Stagger")]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Edge", controls: [.slider("margin")]),
    ]

    /// The most grid positions tried, so a typo ("spacing 0.01") gives a message instead of a hang.
    static let maximumCandidates = 200_000

    static let nothingPicked = "Pick a face for this pattern to fill."
    static let unreadablePick = "This pattern's picked face can't be read. Pick the face again."
    static let noMatch = "The picked face isn't on this solid any more. Pick it again."
    static let notFlat = "Face Pattern works on flat faces for now."
    static let unnamedPick = "The picked face has no stable name, so the pattern may move to a different face "
        + "when the model changes. Pick it again after the change."
    static let mergedPick = "The picked face was merged with a face from another part of the model that has since changed, "
        + "so it isn't clear which part was picked. The pattern fills the closest one; pick the face again to settle it."

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let solid = try inputs.solid("solid")
        let (spacingX, spacingY) = (try inputs.number("spacingX"), try inputs.number("spacingY"))
        guard spacingX.isFinite, spacingX > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“spacingX” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard spacingY.isFinite, spacingY > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“spacingY” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        let margin = try LayoutChecks.number(try inputs.number("margin"), "margin")
        let stagger = try inputs.bool("stagger")
        let (face, warnings) = try pickedFace(of: solid, context: context)
        let frame = try Frame(of: face)
        let region = try FaceRegion.outline(of: face, in: solid.topology, origin: frame.origin, xAxis: frame.xAxis, yAxis: frame.yAxis)
        guard let box = region.bounds else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(FaceRegion.unreadable) }
        // Rows j and columns i of the lattice through the centroid, as far as the face's box reaches.
        let rows = Int((box.min.y / spacingY).rounded(.up))...Int((box.max.y / spacingY).rounded(.down))
        let shift = stagger ? spacingX / 2 : 0
        // Every row counts its columns from this one (a staggered row starts up to `shift` further right, never further left).
        let firstColumn = Int(((box.min.x - shift) / spacingX).rounded(.up))
        let columnsPerRow = (box.max.x - box.min.x + shift) / spacingX + 1
        guard Double(rows.count) * columnsPerRow <= Double(maximumCandidates) else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Face Pattern would have to try more than \(maximumCandidates.display) positions: "
                + "raise the spacing.")
        }
        var rowBranches: [DataTree] = []
        var total = 0
        for row in rows {
            let offset = abs(row) % 2 == 1 ? shift : 0
            let lastColumn = Int(((box.max.x - offset) / spacingX).rounded(.down))
            var cells: [DataTree] = []
            var column = firstColumn
            while column <= lastColumn {
                let local = Vector2(Double(column) * spacingX + offset, Double(row) * spacingY)
                if region.allows(local, margin: margin) {
                    total += 1
                    try PatternLimit.check(total)
                    cells.append(.list([LayoutChecks.placement(frame.point(local), normal: frame.normal, xAxis: frame.xAxis)]))
                } else {
                    cells.append(.list([]))
                }
                column += 1
            }
            rowBranches.append(DataTree(depth: 2, branches: cells) ?? .empty(depth: 2))
        }
        let tree = DataTree(depth: 3, branches: rowBranches) ?? .empty(depth: 3)
        return NodeOutputs(trees: ["placements": tree], warnings: warnings)
    }

    /// The area placements may stand in, for the viewport (patterns spec §8): the edge of the picked face moved `margin` into it,
    /// as 3D closed polylines (the first point not repeated). Empty when the face can't be found or read, which the node itself
    /// says in words. Exact on straight edges; a guide at corners and arcs (`FaceRegion.insetOutline`).
    public static func clippedArea(of solid: Solid, pick: FacePick, margin: Double) -> [[Vector3]] {
        guard let face = solid.topology.resolution(of: pick).faces.first, let frame = try? Frame(of: face),
              let region = try? FaceRegion.outline(of: face, in: solid.topology, origin: frame.origin, xAxis: frame.xAxis,
                                                   yAxis: frame.yAxis) else { return [] }
        return region.insetOutline(margin: max(margin, 0)).map { loop in loop.map(frame.point) }
    }

    /// The flat face the `face` setting names on `solid`, and the warnings its pick earns (as Plane from Face's: an ambiguous
    /// pick, several matches, an unnamed face). A pick on a copy a pattern no longer has is not retried on the part around it.
    private static func pickedFace(of solid: Solid, context: EvalContext) throws -> (FaceInfo, [String]) {
        let pick: FacePick
        switch context.node.inputValues[NodeSetting.face] {
        case nil: throw NodeError.invalidValue(nothingPicked)
        case .facePick(let stored)?: pick = stored
        case .some: throw NodeError.invalidValue(unreadablePick)
        }
        if let gone = solid.topology.vanishedInstances(in: pick.tags).first {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Instance \(InstancePath.text(gone)) no longer exists, so the picked face isn't there any more. "
                + "Pick the face again.")
        }
        let resolution = solid.topology.resolution(of: pick)
        guard let face = resolution.faces.first else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(noMatch) }
        guard face.kind == .plane, face.normal?.normalized != nil else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(notFlat) }
        var warnings: [String] = []
        if resolution.isAmbiguous {
            warnings.append(mergedPick)
        } else if resolution.faces.count > 1 {
            warnings.append("The pick matches \(resolution.faces.count.display) faces; the pattern fills the first.")
        }
        if pick.touchesUnnamedFace { warnings.append(unnamedPick) }
        return (face, warnings)
    }

    /// The face's own coordinates: its centroid, outward normal and the axes Plane from Face would give.
    struct Frame {
        let origin: Vector3
        let normal: Vector3
        let xAxis: Vector3
        let yAxis: Vector3

        init(of face: FaceInfo) throws {
            guard face.kind == .plane, let normal = face.normal?.normalized else { throw NodeError.invalidValue(FacePatternNode.notFlat) }
            let plane = FacePlane.plane(origin: face.centroid, normal: normal)
            (origin, self.normal, xAxis, yAxis) = (face.centroid, normal, plane.xAxis, plane.yAxis)
        }

        func point(_ local: Vector2) -> Vector3 { origin + xAxis * local.x + yAxis * local.y }
    }
}
```

### Task 10: Layout previews in the viewport

**Files:**
- Create: `Sources/CreatorApp/LayoutPreview.swift`, `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+LayoutPreview.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorViewport/Overlay/OverlayTint.swift`, `Sources/CreatorViewport/Render/ViewportPalette+Overlay.swift`, `Sources/CreatorViewport/View/ViewportModel+TextColors.swift`, `Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+Scene.swift`
- Test: `Tests/CreatorViewportTests/OverlayAxisTintTests.swift`, `Tests/CreatorAppTests/LayoutPreviewTests.swift` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the five layout nodes (Tasks 6 to 9), `FacePatternNode.clippedArea`, `ViewportOverlay` / `OverlayLine` (the sketch editor's mechanism: world-space lines, never in the ID pass, never in the framing), `AppModel.editor.selection` / `levelResults`.
- Produces: `OverlayTint.axisX / .axisY / .axisZ` (the theme's axis colours, also for overlay label text); `LayoutPreview.overlay(for:graph:results:) -> ViewportOverlay`, `.layoutTypes`, `.triads(of:)`, `.triadLength(of:)`; `AppModel.layoutOverlay`; the app shows the overlay of the one selected layout node (not while a sketch is open or a pick is running) and `applyPreview` evaluates a selected layout node even in Final preview, so the triads show before anything is wired to it.

Why an overlay: spec §8 asks for guides that "never pick, frame or open a menu" like Final preview's selected-rule edges. Those are guide *items* (they need a solid). Triads aren't geometry of the model, and the overlay already is "drawn over the scene, in no ID pass, in no framing" (`OverlayFill`'s doc, `ViewportOverlay`), which is exactly the requirement; no MetalUI feature is involved. Triad size, tints and the dashed outline are User decision 9.

```swift
import CreatorGeometry

/// The layout previews' triads (patterns spec §8) are drawn in the theme's axis colours, as the view cube's triad is.
struct OverlayAxisTintTests {
    @Test func theAxisTintsAreTheThemesAxisColours() {
        for theme in [ColorTheme.dracula, .alucard, .nord] {
            let palette = ViewportPalette(theme)
            #expect(palette.color(.axisX) == palette.axisX && palette.color(.axisY) == palette.axisY
                && palette.color(.axisZ) == palette.axisZ, "\(theme.name)")
        }
        #expect(ViewportPalette.dracula.color(.axisX) != ViewportPalette.dracula.color(.axisY))

        let palette = ViewportPalette.dracula
        let overlay = ViewportOverlay(lines: [
            OverlayLine(.zero, Vector3(5, 0, 0), tint: .axisX), OverlayLine(.zero, Vector3(0, 5, 0), tint: .axisY),

        let instances = OverlayGeometry.instances(overlay, grid: nil, millimetresPerPoint: 1, scale: 1, palette: palette)

        #expect(instances.map(\.color) == [palette.axisX, palette.axisY, palette.axisZ])

        #expect(model.textColor(for: .axisX) == model.theme.colors.axisX.color)
    }
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import CreatorViewport
import Testing
@testable import CreatorApp

/// A selected layout node shows its placements as small axis triads (patterns spec §8), Face Pattern also its clipped area:
/// an overlay, so nothing about it picks, frames or opens a menu. What it looks like is a human check (group PL).
@MainActor
struct LayoutPreviewTests {
    func plane(_ x: Double) -> Plane { Plane(origin: Vector3(x, 0, 0), normal: .unitZ, xAxis: .unitX) }

    // MARK: Triads

    @Test func eachPlacementGetsAnXAYAndAZLineInTheAxisTints() {
        let lines = LayoutPreview.triads(of: [plane(0), plane(10)])
        #expect(lines.count == 6)
        #expect(lines.prefix(3).map(\.tint) == [.axisX, .axisY, .axisZ])
        #expect(lines[0].a == .zero && lines[0].b == Vector3(4, 0, 0), "along x, a share of the 10 mm between placements")
        #expect(lines[1].b == Vector3(0, 4, 0) && lines[2].b == Vector3(0, 0, 4))
        #expect(lines[3].a == Vector3(10, 0, 0))
    }

    @Test func aLoneTriadHasAFixedSizeAndPlacementsInOnePlaceDoNotCollapseIt() {
        #expect(LayoutPreview.triadLength(of: [plane(0)]) == LayoutPreview.singleTriad)
        #expect(LayoutPreview.triadLength(of: [plane(3), plane(3)]) == LayoutPreview.singleTriad)
        #expect(LayoutPreview.triadLength(of: []) == LayoutPreview.singleTriad)
    }

    @Test func triadsAreSizedByTheClosestNeighboursNotTheRowBreaks() {
        // Two rows of two, 10 apart in a row but 100 between the rows.
        let planes = [plane(0), plane(10), Plane(origin: Vector3(10, 100, 0), normal: .unitZ, xAxis: .unitX),
                      Plane(origin: Vector3(20, 100, 0), normal: .unitZ, xAxis: .unitX),
        ]
        #expect(LayoutPreview.triadLength(of: planes) == 4)
    }

    // MARK: In the app

    func linearApp(_ settings: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) async -> (AppModel, Node, Node) {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let layout = builder.add(LinearPatternNode.self, settings)
        let other = builder.add(NumberNode.self)
        return (await makeApp(builder.graph), layout, other)
    }

    @Test func aSelectedLayoutNodeShowsItsPlacementsEvenBeforeAnythingIsWiredToIt() async throws {
        let (app, layout, _) = await linearApp(["count": .integer(4), "spacing": .number(10)])
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty, "nothing selected")
        app.editor.selection = [layout.id]
        await app.settle()
        let lines = app.viewport.overlay.lines
        #expect(lines.count == 12 && Set(lines.map(\.tint)) == [.axisX, .axisY, .axisZ])
        #expect(app.viewport.items.isEmpty, "the triads are no part of the scene: nothing to pick, frame or open a menu on")
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.points.isEmpty && app.viewport.overlay.fills.isEmpty && app.viewport.overlay.gridPlane == nil)
    }

    @Test func theTriadsFollowAnEditAndGoWhenAnotherNodeOrNothingIsSelected() async throws {
        let (app, layout, other) = await linearApp(["count": .integer(2)])
        app.editor.selection = [layout.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.lines.count == 6)
        try app.document.perform(.setInput(layout.id, "count", .integer(5)))
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.lines.count == 15)
        app.editor.selection = [other.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty)
        app.editor.selection = [layout.id, other.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty, "only one selected node is previewed")
        app.editor.selection = [layout.id]
        await app.settle()
        app.editor.selection = []
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aLayoutNodeInErrorShowsNothing() async throws {
        let (app, layout, _) = await linearApp(["count": .integer(-1)])
        app.editor.selection = [layout.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func theShortcutsAndPlaceShowNothingOfTheirOwn() async throws {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let place = builder.add(PlaceNode.self)
        let app = await makeApp(builder.graph)
        app.editor.selection = [place.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.isEmpty)
    }

    @Test func aCircularPatternShowsOneTriadPerPlacementInEveryRing() async throws {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let ring = builder.add(CircularPatternNode.self, ["count": .integer(6), "rings": .integer(2)])
        let app = await makeApp(builder.graph)
        app.editor.selection = [ring.id]
        await app.settle()
        #expect(app.viewport.overlay.lines.count == 36)
    }

    // MARK: Face Pattern

    @Test func aFacePatternAlsoOutlinesTheAreaItFillsWithDashedLines() async throws {
        var builder = GraphBuilder()
        let plate = builder.box(distance: 6, at: .zero)
        let pattern = builder.add(FacePatternNode.self, ["margin": .number(3)])
        builder.wire(plate.extrude, "solid", to: pattern, "solid")
        var graph = builder.graph
        graph.nodes[plate.rectangle.id]?.inputValues["width"] = .number(60)
        graph.nodes[plate.rectangle.id]?.inputValues["height"] = .number(40)
        // Pick the plate's top face on a first run, as the face menu would.
        let solid = try await solid(of: plate.extrude, in: graph)
        let top = try #require(solid.topology.faces.first { $0.kind == .plane && $0.normal == .unitZ })
        graph.nodes[pattern.id]?.inputValues[NodeSetting.face] = .facePick(try #require(solid.topology.facePick(for: top.id)))
        let app = await makeApp(graph, kernel: OCCTKernel())
        app.editor.selection = [pattern.id]
        await app.settle()
        let lines = app.viewport.overlay.lines
        let dashed = lines.filter(\.isDashed)
        #expect(dashed.count == 4 && dashed.allSatisfy { $0.tint == .construction })
        let corners = Set(dashed.map { Vector2(($0.a.x * 1e3).rounded() / 1e3, ($0.a.y * 1e3).rounded() / 1e3) })
        #expect(corners == [Vector2(-27, -17), Vector2(27, -17), Vector2(27, 17), Vector2(-27, 17)], "\(corners)")
        #expect(dashed.allSatisfy { abs($0.a.z - 6) < 1e-6 && abs($0.b.z - 6) < 1e-6 }, "on the face, 6 mm up")
        let triads = lines.filter { !$0.isDashed }
        #expect(!triads.isEmpty && triads.count % 3 == 0, "and the triads of its placements")
    }

    func solid(of node: Node, in graph: Graph) async throws -> Solid {
        let report = try await Evaluator(registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: OCCTKernel()).evaluate(graph, demand: [node.id])
        return try #require(report.results[node.id]?.outputs?["solid"]?.items.compactMap { scalar -> Solid? in
            if case .solid(let solid) = scalar { solid } else { nil }
        }.first)
    }
}
```

```swift
extension ViewportPalette {
    // One case per tint, nothing to branch on.
    // swiftlint:disable cyclomatic_complexity
    /// An overlay tint's colour (sketcher spec §8's table, and the axis colours of the layout previews).
    func color(_ tint: OverlayTint) -> SIMD4<Float> {
        switch tint {
        case .underConstrained: sketchUnderConstrained
        case .fullyConstrained: sketchFullyConstrained
        case .conflicting: sketchConflicting
        case .construction: sketchConstruction
        case .projected: sketchProjected
        case .selected: sketchSelected
        case .hovered: hover
        case .preview: sketchPreview
        case .region: sketchRegion
        case .axisX: axisX
        case .axisY: axisY
        case .axisZ: axisZ
        }
    }
    // swiftlint:enable cyclomatic_complexity
}
```

`Sources/CreatorApp/LayoutPreview.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorViewport

/// What a selected layout node draws in the viewport (patterns spec §8): its placements as small axis triads (x, y and z in
/// the theme's axis colours), and for Face Pattern the outline of the area it fills, dashed. It is an overlay, drawn over the
/// scene like the sketch editor's lines, so it never picks, frames the view or opens a menu (it is no part of the model).
public enum LayoutPreview {
    /// The nodes whose placements are previewed.
    public static let layoutTypes: Set<String> = [
        LinearPatternNode.typeID, GridPatternNode.typeID, CircularPatternNode.typeID, CurvePatternNode.typeID, FacePatternNode.typeID,
    ]

    /// How long a triad's axes are when there is a single placement, in millimetres.
    static let singleTriad = 5.0
    /// A triad's axes are this much of the distance between neighbouring placements, so triads never touch.
    static let triadShare = 0.4

    /// The overlay for `node`: its `placements` output as triads, with Face Pattern's clipped area. Empty for any other node,
    /// for one that has no good result, and for one with no placements.
    public static func overlay(for node: Node, graph: Graph, results: [NodeID: NodeResult]) -> ViewportOverlay {
        guard layoutTypes.contains(node.typeID), let result = results[node.id], result.state.isSuccess,
              let value = result.outputs?["placements"] else { return ViewportOverlay() }
        let planes = value.items.compactMap { scalar -> Plane? in
            if case .plane(let plane) = scalar { plane } else { nil }
        }
        var overlay = ViewportOverlay(lines: triads(of: planes))
        if node.typeID == FacePatternNode.typeID {
            overlay.lines += clippedArea(of: node, graph: graph, results: results)
        }
        return overlay
    }

    /// An x, y and z line for each placement.
    static func triads(of planes: [Plane]) -> [OverlayLine] {
        let length = triadLength(of: planes)
        return planes.flatMap { plane in
            [OverlayLine(plane.origin, plane.origin + plane.xAxis * length, tint: .axisX),
             OverlayLine(plane.origin, plane.origin + plane.yAxis * length, tint: .axisY),
             OverlayLine(plane.origin, plane.origin + plane.normal * length, tint: .axisZ),
            ]
        }
    }

    /// `singleTriad` for one placement (or placements all in one place), else `triadShare` of the smallest distance between
    /// placements that follow each other.
    static func triadLength(of planes: [Plane]) -> Double {
        let steps = zip(planes, planes.dropFirst()).map { ($1.origin - $0.origin).length }.filter { $0 > 1e-9 }
        guard let nearest = steps.min() else { return singleTriad }
        return nearest * triadShare
    }

    /// The dashed outline of the area a Face Pattern fills: its solid and margin read the way the node reads them (from the
    /// wire, else the typed value), its face the node's pick.
    static func clippedArea(of node: Node, graph: Graph, results: [NodeID: NodeResult]) -> [OverlayLine] {
        guard case .facePick(let pick)? = node.inputValues[NodeSetting.face],
              case .solid(let solid)? = input("solid", of: node, graph: graph, results: results)?.items.first else { return [] }
        var margin = 0.0
        if case .number(let value)? = input("margin", of: node, graph: graph, results: results)?.items.first { margin = value }
        return FacePatternNode.clippedArea(of: solid, pick: pick, margin: margin).flatMap { loop in
            loop.indices.map { index in
                OverlayLine(loop[index], loop[(index + 1) % loop.count], tint: .construction, isDashed: true)
            }
        }
    }

    /// The value on `socket` of `node`: what the wire into it carries, else its typed value converted as the evaluator would.
    private static func input(_ socket: SocketName, of node: Node, graph: Graph, results: [NodeID: NodeResult]) -> Value? {
        if let link = graph.incomingLink(to: Endpoint(node: node.id, socket: socket)) {
            return results[link.from.node]?.outputs?[link.from.socket]
        }
        let spec = FacePatternNode.inputs.first { $0.name == socket }
        guard let constant = node.inputValues[socket] ?? spec?.defaultValue, let type = spec?.type else { return nil }
        return Scalar(constant)?.converted(to: type).map(Value.one)
    }
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGraph

extension AppModel {
    /// The layout preview for the one node selected in the level shown (`LayoutPreview`), or nothing: while picking, the
    /// sketch editor draws its own overlay, so this is never asked then.
    var layoutOverlay: ViewportOverlay {
        guard editor.selection.count == 1, let id = editor.selection.first, let node = editor.graph.nodes[id] else {
            return ViewportOverlay()
        }
        return LayoutPreview.overlay(for: node, graph: editor.graph, results: editor.levelResults)
    }
}
```

`Sources/CreatorApp/AppModel+PatternOnFace.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorViewport

extension AppModel {
    /// "Pattern on Face" (patterns spec §3): a Face Pattern holding the face's remembered pick, wired from the node that made the
    /// face's solid and placed beside it, as one undo step, and selected so its placements show (`LayoutPreview`). A face that
    /// isn't flat is said in words, a face of a solid that is gone does nothing. Inside a group it is refused, as New Sketch on
    /// Face is: the pick would have to be written relative to the group (groups Errata (C2)).
    func patternOnFace(_ ref: ViewportFaceRef, _ pick: FacePick) {
        guard viewport.items.indices.contains(ref.solidIndex) else { return }
        guard !editor.isInsideGroup else {
            alert = .problem(AppProblem("No pattern was made", "Patterns on a face can't be made inside a group yet. "
                                        + "Make it before grouping, or from the top level."))
            return
        }
        let solid = viewport.items[ref.solidIndex].solid
        guard let face = solid.topology.face(ref.face), PlaneFromFaceNode.plane(of: face) != nil else {
            alert = .problem(AppProblem("No pattern was made", "Only a flat face can hold a pattern."))
            return
        }
        guard let source = producer(of: solid) else {
            alert = .problem(AppProblem("No pattern was made", "The node that made this solid can't be found."))
            return
        }
        let from = document.graph.nodes[source.node]?.position ?? .zero
        var pattern = registry.makeNode(FacePatternNode.typeID, at: from + Vector2(240, 140))
        pattern.inputValues[NodeSetting.face] = .facePick(pick)
        let commands: [GraphCommand] = [
            .addNode(pattern), .connect(Link(from: source, to: Endpoint(node: pattern.id, socket: "solid"))),
        ]
        do {
            try document.perform(.batch(commands), name: UndoName.patternOnFace)
        } catch {
            alert = .problem(AppProblem("No pattern was made", error.message))
            return
        }
        editor.selection = [pattern.id]
    }
}
```

### Task 12: The acceptance test (spec §9)

**Files:**
- Test: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/PatternsAcceptanceTests.swift` (new), `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Support/LegacyDocuments.swift` (Task 5's file gains the format-5 bracket)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above, `LegacyDocuments` (Task 5), `DocumentModel` (`fileData()`, `init(data:)`, `perform`, `waitForEvaluation`), `OCCTKernel` (`export` to STEP, `OCCTShape.readSTEP`).
- Produces: nothing; this is the end-to-end proof.

The part: a 120 × 80 × 8 plate; a Circular Pattern of two rings (4 holes at radius 15, 8 at 25) into a through-all Hole Pattern whose diameter is a Graft of Series `[6, 4]` (Ø6 in ring 0, Ø4 in ring 1); a staggered Face Pattern (14 × 12, margin 6) on the drilled plate's top face, which keeps clear of the bolt holes, into a Slot Pattern (vents); an Edges by Tag picking the rim of bolt hole `{1;3}`, a Fillet and an Output. The picks are made the way the app makes them. The tests: build and check every instance by path and the per-ring bore areas; change the bolt radius, the ring count (2 to 3), the vent spacing and the margin; save (format 6), reopen, every pick holds and the volume is the same; STEP export succeeds and reads back; the same file marked format 5 opens unchanged; **a bracket that format 5 really wrote** (`LegacyDocuments.bracketFormat5`, captured by running commit `187b5ff~1`, before the tree nodes) opens with no warning, its seven chamfer picks select seven edges and its part has the volume, face count and edge count it had then, and saved again is format 6 and the same part; **raising the margin from 6 to 12 mm drops vents but every vent that stays keeps its path, its position and the names of its faces**; a ring count that drops `{1;3}` makes the rule warn naming it; turning the bolt circle keeps the fillet on the same hole.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/PatternsAcceptanceTests.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CreatorOCCT

/// Patterns spec §9, headless, in the spirit of the bracket: a 120 × 80 × 8 plate with a circular bolt pattern in two rings
/// whose holes are a diameter per ring, a staggered vent grid of slots on its top face with an edge margin (the Face Pattern
/// keeps clear of the bolt holes too), and a fillet on one patterned hole, the one at `{1;3}`. Parameters change, the file is
/// saved (format 6) and reopened, every pick holds, STEP export succeeds, and the same file marked format 5 opens unchanged.
@MainActor
struct PatternsAcceptanceTests {
    struct Part {
        var graph: Graph
        let ring: Node, bolts: Node, vents: Node, slots: Node, rule: Node, fillet: Node, output: Node
    }

    /// Plate → Hole Pattern (bolt circle, Ø6 then Ø4 by ring) → Slot Pattern (vents, at a staggered Face Pattern of the
    /// drilled plate's top) → Edges by Tag (the rim of the bolt hole `{1;3}`) → Fillet → Output.
    static func makePart(kernel: any Kernel) async throws -> Part {
        var h = Harness()
        let plate = h.box(120, 80, 8, at: Vector3(0, 0, -8))
        let ring = h.add(CircularPatternNode.self, ["radius": .number(15), "count": .integer(4), "countStep": .integer(4),
                                                    "rings": .integer(2), "radiusStep": .number(10),
        ])
        let diameters = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["start": .number(6), "step": .number(-2), "count": .integer(2)])
        let perRing = h.add(GraftNode.self)
        let bolts = h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["throughAll": .bool(true)])
        h.wire(plate, "solid", to: bolts, "part")
        h.wire(ring, "placements", to: bolts, "placements")
        h.wire(diameters, "values", to: perRing, "tree")
        h.wire(perRing, "tree", to: bolts, "diameter")
        let vents = h.add(FacePatternNode.self, ["spacingX": .number(14), "spacingY": .number(12), "stagger": .bool(true),
                                                 "margin": .number(6),
        ])
        h.wire(bolts, "solid", to: vents, "solid")
        let slots = h.add(SlotPatternNode.self, ["length": .number(8), "width": .number(3), "throughAll": .bool(true)])
        h.wire(bolts, "solid", to: slots, "part")
        h.wire(vents, "placements", to: slots, "placements")
        let rule = h.add(EdgesByTagNode.self)
        h.wire(slots, "solid", to: rule, "solid")
        let fillet = h.add(FilletNode.self, ["radius": .number(0.5)])
        h.wire(rule, "edges", to: fillet, "edges")
        let output = h.add(OutputNode.self)
        h.wire(fillet, "solid", to: output, "solid")

        // The picks, made the way the app makes them: the top face of the drilled plate, then the rim of hole {1;3} on the result.
        let drilled = try onlySolid(try await h.run([bolts], kernel: kernel), bolts)
        let top = try #require(drilled.topology.faces.filter { $0.kind == .plane && $0.normal == .unitZ }
            .max { $0.centroid.z < $1.centroid.z })
        h.set(vents, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(try #require(drilled.topology.facePick(for: top.id))))
        let slotted = try onlySolid(try await h.run([slots], kernel: kernel), slots)
        let bore = TopoTag(node: NodeID.instanceScoped([bolts.id, bolts.id]), item: try #require(InstancePath.item(for: [1, 3])),
                           role: .side(segment: 1))
        let capTag = TopoTag(node: plate.id, item: 0, role: .endCap)
        let rim = try #require(slotted.topology.edges.first { edge in
            guard edge.kind == .circle, !edge.isSeam, edge.faces.count == 2 else { return false }
            let sides = edge.faces.compactMap { slotted.topology.face($0) }
            return sides.contains { $0.tags.contains(bore) } && sides.contains { $0.tags.contains(capTag) }
        })
        h.set(rule, NodeSetting.picks, .edgePicks(slotted.topology.picks(for: [rim.id])))
        return Part(graph: h.graph, ring: ring, bolts: bolts, vents: vents, slots: slots, rule: rule, fillet: fillet, output: output)
    }

    func expectAllOK(_ document: DocumentModel, _ when: String, sourceLocation: SourceLocation = #_sourceLocation) {
        let problems = document.results.compactMap { id, result -> String? in
            if case .ok = result.state { return nil }
            return "\(document.graph.nodes[id]?.name ?? "?"): \(result.state)"
        }
        #expect(problems.isEmpty, "\(when): \(problems.sorted().joined(separator: "; "))", sourceLocation: sourceLocation)
    }

    func finalSolid(_ document: DocumentModel, _ part: Part) throws -> Solid {
        try #require(document.results[part.output.id]?.outputs?["solid"]?.solids?.first)
    }

    /// The paths of the instances of `node` that have faces in `solid`.
    func paths(of node: Node, in solid: Solid) -> Set<[Int]> {
        let qualifier = NodeID.instanceScoped([node.id, node.id])
        let items = Set(solid.topology.faces.flatMap(\.tags).filter { $0.node == qualifier }.map(\.item))
        return Set(items.compactMap { InstancePath.indices(ofItem: $0) })
    }

    /// Whether the fillet's blend faces name the bore of the hole at `path`.
    func isFilleted(_ solid: Solid, hole path: [Int], bolts: Node) -> Bool {
        let bore = TopoTag(node: NodeID.instanceScoped([bolts.id, bolts.id]), item: InstancePath.item(for: path) ?? -1,
                           role: .side(segment: 1))
        return solid.topology.faces.contains { face in
            face.tags.contains { tag in
                if case .blend(let edge) = tag.role { edge.first.union(edge.second).contains(bore) } else { false }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test func theWholePartBuildsEditsSavesReopensAndExports() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let part = try await Self.makePart(kernel: kernel)
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.graph), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "as built")
        let built = try finalSolid(document, part)
        // Two rings of 4 and 8 bolt holes, each hole named by its ring and place; the vents are Face Pattern's rows.
        #expect(paths(of: part.bolts, in: built).count == 12 && paths(of: part.bolts, in: built).allSatisfy { $0.count == 2 })
        let placements = try #require(document.results[part.vents.id]?.outputs?["placements"]?.asTree)
        let ventPaths = Set(placements.leaves.flatMap { leaf in leaf.items.indices.map { leaf.path.indices + [$0] } })
        #expect(paths(of: part.slots, in: built) == ventPaths && ventPaths.count > 10, "\(ventPaths.count) vents")
        #expect(isFilleted(built, hole: [1, 3], bolts: part.bolts))
        // Per-ring diameters: the bores of ring 0 are Ø6 and ring 1's Ø4, through 8 mm of plate (the filleted hole {1;3} aside).
        let bolt = NodeID.instanceScoped([part.bolts.id, part.bolts.id])
        let bores = built.topology.faces.filter { $0.kind == .cylinder && $0.tags.contains { $0.node == bolt } }
        func areas(ring: Int) -> [Double] {
            bores.filter { face in face.tags.contains { InstancePath.indices(ofItem: $0.item)?.first == ring && $0.node == bolt } }
                .filter { face in !face.tags.contains { $0.item == InstancePath.item(for: [1, 3]) } }.map(\.area)
        }
        #expect(areas(ring: 0).count == 4 && areas(ring: 0).allSatisfy { isClose($0, Double.pi * 6 * 8, relative: 1e-4) })
        #expect(areas(ring: 1).count == 7 && areas(ring: 1).allSatisfy { isClose($0, Double.pi * 4 * 8, relative: 1e-4) })

        // Change the pattern: a bigger bolt circle with a third ring, wider vents, a smaller margin.
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.ring.id, "radius", .number(16)))
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.ring.id, "rings", .integer(3)))
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.vents.id, "spacingX", .number(15)))
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.vents.id, "margin", .number(5)))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "after the edits")
        let edited = try finalSolid(document, part)
        #expect(paths(of: part.bolts, in: edited).count == 24, "4 + 8 + 12 holes")
        #expect(isFilleted(edited, hole: [1, 3], bolts: part.bolts), "the fillet is still on the hole at {1;3}")
        #expect(document.results[part.rule.id]?.state.isSuccess == true)

        // Save and reopen: format 6, every pick holds.
        let data = try document.fileData()
        let text = try #require(String(bytes: data, encoding: .utf8))
        #expect(text.contains(#""formatVersion" : 6"#))
        let reopened = try DocumentModel(data: data, registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await reopened.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(reopened, "reopened")
        let again = try finalSolid(reopened, part)
        #expect(isFilleted(again, hole: [1, 3], bolts: part.bolts))
        #expect(isClose(try await volume(again, kernel), try await volume(edited, kernel)))
        #expect(paths(of: part.slots, in: again) == paths(of: part.slots, in: edited))

        // STEP export of the finished part succeeds and reads back.
        let step = URL.temporaryDirectory.appending(path: "patterns-\(UUID().uuidString).step")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: step) }
        try await kernel.export([again], format: .step, to: step)
        let readVolume = try OCCTKernel.serialized { () throws -> Double in try OCCTShape.readSTEP(step).properties().volume }
        #expect(isClose(readVolume, try await volume(again, kernel), relative: 1e-3))

        // The same file marked format 5 opens and evaluates the same: nothing in it needs 6 (it holds no Path Mapper).
        let asFive = text.replacing(#""formatVersion" : 6"#, with: #""formatVersion" : 5"#)
        let older = try DocumentModel(data: Data(asFive.utf8), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await older.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(older, "format 5")
        #expect(isClose(try await volume(try finalSolid(older, part), kernel), try await volume(edited, kernel)))
    }

    /// Spec §9, "a format-5 file opens unchanged": `LegacyDocuments.bracketFormat5` is the bracket as the program wrote it before
    /// the tree nodes (commit 187b5ff~1: format 5, no pattern node, the chamfer's seven picked edges). Opened now it evaluates with
    /// no warning, its picks select what they selected and the part is the part it was; saved again it is format 6 and unchanged.
    @Test func aBracketWrittenAsFormat5OpensWithItsPicksAndVolumeUnchanged() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        #expect(LegacyDocuments.bracketFormat5.contains(#""formatVersion":5"#))
        let document = try DocumentModel(data: Data(LegacyDocuments.bracketFormat5.utf8), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "format 5")
        let nodes = document.graph.nodes.values
        let output = try #require(nodes.first { $0.typeID == OutputNode.typeID })
        let rule = try #require(nodes.first { $0.typeID == EdgesByTagNode.typeID })
        func part(_ document: DocumentModel) throws -> Solid {
            try #require(document.results[output.id]?.outputs?["solid"]?.solids?.first)
        }
        let opened = try part(document)
        #expect(isClose(try await volume(opened, kernel), 25183.435985523505, relative: 1e-9))
        #expect(opened.topology.faces.count == 25 && opened.topology.edges.count == 67)
        #expect(document.results[rule.id]?.outputs?["edges"]?.edgeSets?.first?.edges.count == 7, "the seven picked edges")

        let text = try #require(String(bytes: try document.fileData(), encoding: .utf8))
        #expect(text.contains(#""formatVersion" : 6"#))
        let saved = try DocumentModel(data: Data(text.utf8), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await saved.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(saved, "format 5 saved as 6")
        #expect(isClose(try await volume(try part(saved), kernel), try await volume(opened, kernel), relative: 1e-9))
    }

    /// Patterns spec §6: "changing spacing, position or size keeps paths". Raising the margin from 6 to 12 mm drops the vents near
    /// the edge and the bolt holes; every vent that stays keeps its path, its place on the plate and the names of its faces, so a
    /// pick on it would still be on it.
    @Test func aVentKeepsItsPathAndPlaceWhenTheMarginGrows() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let part = try await Self.makePart(kernel: kernel)
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.graph), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "as built")
        func vents() throws -> [[Int]: Vector3] {
            let tree = try #require(document.results[part.vents.id]?.outputs?["placements"]?.asTree)
            var found: [[Int]: Vector3] = [:]
            for leaf in tree.leaves {
                for (index, item) in leaf.items.enumerated() {
                    if case .plane(let plane) = item { found[leaf.path.indices + [index]] = plane.origin }
                }
            }
            return found
        }
        /// The centres of the faces that name the vent at `path`.
        func faceCentres(_ path: [Int]) throws -> [Vector3] {
            let solid = try finalSolid(document, part)
            let qualifier = NodeID.instanceScoped([part.slots.id, part.slots.id])
            let item = try #require(InstancePath.item(for: path))
            return solid.topology.faces.filter { $0.tags.contains { $0.node == qualifier && $0.item == item } }.map(\.centroid)
        }
        func isAtItsPlace(_ path: [Int], _ origin: Vector3) throws -> Bool {
            let centres = try faceCentres(path)
            return !centres.isEmpty && centres.allSatisfy { Vector2($0.x - origin.x, $0.y - origin.y).length < 5 }
        }

        let before = try vents()
        for (path, origin) in before { #expect(try isAtItsPlace(path, origin), "as built, vent \(path)") }
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.vents.id, "margin", .number(12)))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "a bigger margin")
        let after = try vents()
        #expect(!after.isEmpty && after.count < before.count, "\(before.count) vents became \(after.count)")
        for (path, origin) in after {
            let was = try #require(before[path], "vent \(path) was not there before")
            #expect((was - origin).length < 1e-6, "vent \(path) moved")
            #expect(try isAtItsPlace(path, origin), "vent \(path)'s faces are no longer at it")
        }
    }

    @Test func aPickOnARingThatIsGoneWarnsNamingItsPath() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let part = try await Self.makePart(kernel: kernel)
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.graph), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.ring.id, "rings", .integer(1)))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        guard case .warning(let message)? = document.results[part.rule.id]?.state else {
            Issue.record("the rule should warn, got \(String(describing: document.results[part.rule.id]?.state))")
            return
        }
        #expect(message == "Instance {1;3} no longer exists, so the edge picked on it isn't selected.")
    }

    @Test func rotatingTheBoltCircleKeepsEveryHolesPath() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let part = try await Self.makePart(kernel: kernel)
        let document = DocumentModel(file: GraphFile(graph: part.graph), registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        try document.perform(.setInput(part.ring.id, "startAngle", .number(20)))
        await document.waitForEvaluation()
        expectAllOK(document, "rotated")
        let solid = try finalSolid(document, part)
        #expect(isFilleted(solid, hole: [1, 3], bolts: part.bolts), "the same hole, turned with the ring")
    }
}
```

### Task 13: Review-focus tests

**Files:**
- Test: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/LayoutReviewTests.swift` (new), `Tests/CreatorAppTests/GroupViewportTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 6 to 11. Produces nothing.

The Review Focus tests that don't belong to one node: flat and circular layouts name their holes `{i}` and `{ring;i}`; a Circular Pattern into Place makes one kernel call and a tree of copies; an edge that is neither straight nor round refuses naming it; a Face Pattern on the floor of a hole that has gone says which; a list wired into any of the five layouts is refused in that layout's own words (`singleValuesRefusal`, before it runs; User decision 12); Pattern on Face inside a group says why it does nothing.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

`Tests/CreatorNodesTests/LayoutReviewTests.swift`

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Testing

/// The layouts met by the inputs a person is likely to give them (the plan's Review Focus): other nodes' trees, edges that
/// aren't straight or round, a pick on a face of a copy that is gone.
struct LayoutReviewTests {
    @Test func aLinearPatternNamesItsHolesByIndexAndACircularOneByRingAndIndex() async throws {
        for (layout, expected) in [(LinearPatternNode.self as any NodeDefinition.Type, Set([[0], [1], [2], [3]])),
                                   (CircularPatternNode.self, Set([[0, 0], [0, 1], [0, 2], [0, 3]])),
        ] {
            var plate = PatternPlate()
            let placements = plate.h.add(layout, ["count": .integer(4), "rings": .integer(1)])
            let holes = plate.h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["diameter": .number(3), "depth": .number(3)])
            plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: holes, "part")
            plate.h.wire(placements, "placements", to: holes, "placements")
            let report = try await plate.h.run([holes], kernel: OCCTKernel())
            let part = try onlySolid(report, holes)
            let qualifier = NodeID.instanceScoped([holes.id, holes.id])
            let items = Set(part.topology.faces.flatMap(\.tags).filter { $0.node == qualifier }.map(\.item))
            #expect(Set(items.compactMap { InstancePath.indices(ofItem: $0) }) == expected, "\(layout.displayName)")
        }
    }

    /// A layout makes one tree of placements: it doesn't run once per value of a Series, so each says so in its own words
    /// (`NodeDefinition.singleValuesRefusal`), before it does any work.
    @Test func aListWiredIntoALayoutIsRefusedInTheLayoutsOwnWords() async throws {
        let layouts: [(any NodeDefinition.Type, SocketName)] = [
            (LinearPatternNode.self, "spacing"), (GridPatternNode.self, "spacingX"), (CircularPatternNode.self, "radius"),
            (CurvePatternNode.self, "spacing"), (FacePatternNode.self, "spacingX"),
        ]
        for (layout, input) in layouts {
            var plate = PatternPlate()
            let series = plate.h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(3)])
            let node = plate.h.add(layout)
            plate.h.wire(series, "values", to: node, input)
            if layout == FacePatternNode.self { plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: node, "solid") }
            let report = try await plate.h.run([node], kernel: FakeKernel())
            #expect(report.error(node) == "\(layout.displayName) takes single values, not lists: wire one value into each input, "
                + "or pick one out of the list first with List Item.", "\(layout.displayName)")
        }
    }

    @Test func aCircularPatternIntoPlaceMakesOneKernelCallAndATreeOfCopies() async throws {
        var plate = PatternPlate()
        let rings = plate.h.add(CircularPatternNode.self, ["count": .integer(3), "rings": .integer(2), "countStep": .integer(1)])
        let tool = plate.cylinder(diameter: 2, from: 0, to: 2)
        let place = plate.h.add(PlaceNode.self)
        plate.h.wire(rings, "placements", to: place, "placements")
        plate.h.wire(tool, "solid", to: place, "tool")
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        await kernel.clearLog()
        let report = try await plate.h.run([place], kernel: kernel)
        let tree = try #require(report.value(place, "solids")?.asTree, "\(String(describing: report.state(place)))")
        #expect(tree.leaves.map { $0.items.count } == [3, 4])
        #expect(await kernel.operationLog.filter { $0 == "place" }.count == 1)
    }

    @Test func anEdgeThatIsNeitherStraightNorRoundIsRefusedNamingIt() async throws {
        let box = try await FakeKernel().extrude(.rectangle(width: 10, height: 10, plane: .xy), distance: 5, mode: .oneSided,
                                                 tag: NodeTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0))
        var topology = box.topology
        topology.edges[0].curve = nil
        let solid = Solid(topology: topology, bounds: box.bounds, storage: box.storage)
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(CurvePatternNode.typeID)
        let inputs = NodeInputs(item: 0, slots: [
            "edges": .list([.edgeSet(EdgeSet(solid: solid, edges: [topology.edges[0].id]))]),
            "mode": .item(.integer(0)), "count": .item(.integer(3)), "spacing": .item(.number(10)),
        ])
        let context = EvalContext(node: node, item: 0, parameters: [:])
        let id = topology.edges[0].id.rawValue
        await #expect(throws: NodeError.invalidValue("Curve Pattern follows straight and circular edges for now: "
            + "edge \(id) is another kind of curve.")) {
            _ = try await CurvePatternNode.evaluate(inputs, kernel: FakeKernel(), context: context)
        }
    }

    @Test func aFacePatternOnTheFloorOfAHoleThatIsGoneNamesTheHole() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var plate = PatternPlate()
        let rings = plate.h.add(CircularPatternNode.self, ["count": .integer(4), "radius": .number(10), "rings": .integer(2)])
        let holes = plate.h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["diameter": .number(4), "depth": .number(3)])
        let pattern = plate.h.add(FacePatternNode.self, ["margin": .number(0.5), "spacingX": .number(1), "spacingY": .number(1)])
        plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: holes, "part")
        plate.h.wire(rings, "placements", to: holes, "placements")
        plate.h.wire(holes, "solid", to: pattern, "solid")
        let drilled = try onlySolid(try await plate.h.run([holes], kernel: kernel), holes)
        let qualifier = NodeID.instanceScoped([holes.id, holes.id])
        let item = try #require(InstancePath.item(for: [1, 2]))
        // The floor of hole {1;2}: flat, facing up, three millimetres down, and named by that hole.
        let floor = try #require(drilled.topology.faces.first { face in
            face.kind == .plane && face.normal == .unitZ && abs(face.centroid.z + 3) < 1e-6
                && face.tags.contains { $0.node == qualifier && $0.item == item }
        })
        plate.h.set(pattern, NodeSetting.face, .facePick(try #require(drilled.topology.facePick(for: floor.id))))
        #expect(try await plate.h.run([pattern], kernel: kernel).isOK(pattern))
        plate.h.set(rings, "rings", .integer(1))
        let report = try await plate.h.run([pattern], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.error(pattern) == "Instance {1;2} no longer exists, so the picked face isn't there any more. Pick the face again.")
    }
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:** none (text). No MetalUI gap was found, so `docs/metalui-gaps.md` is not touched.

The script below makes every edit with an exact anchor and stops if an anchor is missing or repeated (so a drifted file is never edited somewhere else). It updates CLAUDE.md (the project-state paragraph, the `CreatorKernel` bullet on tag items, the node count 41 to 46 and "eleven `Patterns` nodes", the Patterns bullet, the `CreatorApp` bullet on previews and Pattern on Face); edits row 7 of the roadmap and adds row 7a-3; appends "Errata (7a-3: layouts)" to the spec; and appends human-check group PL (PL-1 to PL-12: the library, triads, how they read (through the part too), rings and per-ring sizes, Pattern on Face, holes in the face, vents and a pick on one, Curve and Linear, a Boolean over a tree, tree display, old files and groups, a list on a layout).

- [ ] **Step 1: Save the script outside the worktree and run it from the worktree root**

`docs_7a3.py` (a scratch file outside the worktree):

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Task 13: the 7a-3 (layouts) documentation edits. Run from the repository root: python3 docs_7a3.py

Every edit replaces one exact anchor and asserts that it was there exactly once, so a drifted file stops the script
instead of being edited somewhere else."""
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def edit(path, old, new):
    file = Path(path)
    text = file.read_text()
    if text.count(old) != 1:
        sys.exit(f"{path}: expected exactly one occurrence of {old[:60]!r}, found {text.count(old)}")
    file.write_text(text.replace(old, new))
```
